## TERM-REL-NEGATIONS

- Source term or sense: antisymmetric / asymmetric / irreflexive
- Choice: ضد متناظره / نامتناظره / نفي انعکاسي
- Why provisional: provisional qualified labels; no exact attestation
- Rationale: Labels must not collapse three distinct logical conditions. Antisymmetry permits mutual relatedness only at equal points; asymmetry never permits it; irreflexivity excludes every self-pair. Mere failure of symmetry or reflexivity is expressed as a negated sentence, not as these technical labels.
- Alternatives: ضد متقارن; نامتقارن; غير انعکاسي
- Please double-check: In Pakistani Pashto mathematical prose, is «ضد متناظره / نامتناظره / نفي انعکاسي» the clearest conventional rendering of “antisymmetric / asymmetric / irreflexive” while preserving the distinctions in the rationale? If not, what exact replacement and inflected forms should be used?

## TERM-ENUMERATION

- Source term or sense: enumeration / list / to enumerate
- Choice: شمېرنه / لړ / په لړ کښې راوړل
- Why provisional: attested listing concept; exact enumeration noun provisional
- Rationale: Use شمېرنه as the defined enumeration noun, لړ for list, and explicit listing verbs. Every set member must occur at a finite position; repetitions are allowed in the main definition. Alternative bijective definitions must be signposted.
- Alternatives: فهرست کول; لړنه

## TERM-SET-SIZE

- Source term or sense: size / equinumerous
- Choice: اندازه / همشمېره
- Why provisional: general equal-cardinality concept directly attested; chosen همشمېره adjective provisional
- Rationale: Size compares membership cardinalities, not spatial extent. Equinumerosity is not equality of the sets or the earlier equivalence-relation label. Infinite sizes follow source bijection definitions. Later visual inspection of the Bukovský/Niazman cardinality theorem directly supports equality of finite and infinite set sizes through bijections and all three equivalence-relation laws; it does not attest the exact adjective همشمېره.
- Alternatives: کميت; هم ځواک
- Please double-check: In Pakistani Pashto mathematical prose, is «اندازه / همشمېره» the clearest conventional rendering of “size / equinumerous” while preserving the distinctions in the rationale? If not, what exact replacement and inflected forms should be used?

## TERM-DEDEKIND-CUT

- Source term or sense: Dedekind cut / proper initial segment with no greatest element
- Choice: د ډېډېکېنډ پرېکون / ناتشه خاصه پيلنۍ برخه چې تر ټولو لوے غړے نۀ لري

## TERM-ALGEBRAIC-STRUCTURES

- Source term or sense: commutative ring / ordered ring / ordered field; associativity, commutativity, distributivity
- Choice: تبادلي حلقه / مرتبه حلقه / مرتب ميدان؛ اتحادي، تبديلي او توزيعي خاصيتونه
- Why provisional: operation-law labels regionally attested; ring and field compounds provisional
- Rationale: Keep the eight commutative-ring laws, total-order compatibility, multiplicative inverse for nonzero elements, and real completeness logically distinct. The native witness directly supports associative, commutative and distributive properties but does not define these structures.
- Alternatives: تبادلي رینګ او مرتب فیلډ; د عملياتو د قوانينو تشريحي بيان

## TERM-CAUCHY-SEQUENCE

- Source term or sense: Cauchy sequence / limit / tends to zero / convergence
- Choice: د کوشي لړۍ / حد / صفر ته ورنژدې کېږي / تقارب
- Why provisional: sequence, limit and convergence concepts regionally attested; Pakistani surfaces adapted
- Rationale: A Cauchy sequence here is a function from the naturals to the rationals whose sufficiently late values are arbitrarily close to one another. Distinguish this internal Cauchy condition from already having a real-valued limit, since the construction is intended to produce the reals. Use لړۍ for sequence and an explicit approach-to-zero phrase for the null difference.
- Alternatives: د کوشي ترادف; د کوشي ناطقه لړۍ
- Please double-check: In Pakistani Pashto mathematical prose, is «د کوشي لړۍ / حد / صفر ته ورنژدې کېږي / تقارب» the clearest conventional rendering of “Cauchy sequence / limit / tends to zero / convergence” while preserving the distinctions in the rationale? If not, what exact replacement and inflected forms should be used?

## TERM-RECURSIVE

- Source term or sense: recursive definition / mathematical induction
- Choice: بازګشتي تعريف / رياضي استقرا
- Why provisional: mathematical induction directly attested; recursive-definition label remains provisional
- Rationale: Keep recursive definition distinct from proof by induction. Induction has a base case and a successor-preservation step; a recursive definition fixes new values from earlier ones and requires a separate existence-and-uniqueness justification.

## TERM-DEDEKIND-INFINITE

- Source term or sense: Dedekind infinite / Dedekind algebra / successor function / closed under a function
- Choice: د ډېډېکېنډ له مخې نامتناهي / د ډېډېکېنډ الجبر / تالي تابع / د تابعې له مخې بند
- Why provisional: Dedekind finiteness and successor-minimality concepts attested; algebra compound provisional
- Rationale: Dedekind infinite means that the set injects into a proper subset of itself. A Dedekind algebra adds a distinguished initial element, an injective successor missing that element, and the least-set closure condition. Do not infer ordinary cardinal infinitude without the surrounding Choice assumptions when the direction matters.
- Alternatives: د ډېډېکېنډ نامتناهي سټ; د تالي له مخې تړلی الجبر

If not, what exact replacement and inflected forms should be used?

## TERM-PROP-SYNTAX

- Source term or sense: propositional variable / logical connective / negation / conjunction / disjunction / conditional / biconditional / formation sequence
- Choice: بياني متغير / منطقي نښلوونکے / نفي / عطف / فصل / شرطي / دوه اړخيز شرطي / د فارمول جوړښتي لړ
- Why provisional: formation and connective concepts regionally attested; Pakistani surfaces adapted
- Rationale: Use the connective labels consistently and preserve whether a connective is primitive or defined. A formation sequence may contain earlier formulas and even irrelevant entries; only its last entry is required to be the formula it forms.
- Alternatives: قضيې متغير او منطقي رابط; د بيان د انشاء لړ

## TERM-PROP-SEMANTICS

- Source term or sense: truth value / truth table / valuation / evaluation / satisfaction / tautology / semantic entailment / satisfiable / unsatisfiable / contingent
- Choice: د رښتياوالي قيمت / د رښتياوالي جدول / ارزښت ټاکنه / ارزونه / صدق / تاوتولوژي / معنايي استلزام / د صدق وړ / د صدق نۀ وړ / اتفاقي
- Why provisional: core concepts regionally attested; Pakistani labels and distinctions editorially fixed
- Rationale: A valuation assigns truth values to propositional variables; evaluation extends that assignment to all formulas. Satisfaction is truth under a valuation. Tautologies are true under every valuation, contingent formulas under some but not all, and semantic entailment preserves satisfaction from premises to conclusion. Satisfiability requires at least one common satisfying valuation.
- Alternatives: صدق ټاکنه او د حقيقت جدول; اعتبار او مدلول
- Please double-check: In Pakistani Pashto mathematical prose, is «د رښتياوالي قيمت / د رښتياوالي جدول / ارزښت ټاکنه / ارزونه / صدق / تاوتولوژي / معنايي استلزام / د صدق وړ / د صدق نۀ وړ / اتفاقي» the clearest conventional rendering of “truth value / truth table / valuation / evaluation / satisfaction / tautology / semantic entailment / satisfiable / unsatisfiable / contingent” while preserving the distinctions in the rationale? If not, what exact replacement and inflected forms should be used?

## TERM-SUBSTITUTION

- Source term or sense: uniform substitution / simultaneous substitution
- Choice: يو شان ځايناستي / هممهاله ځايناستي
- Why provisional: formula-construction context attested; exact substitution compounds provisional
- Rationale: Uniform substitution replaces every occurrence of one propositional variable by the same formula. Simultaneous substitution applies a listed family at once; do not read it as successive replacement unless a later definition explicitly fixes such an order.
- Alternatives: تعويض; يو ډول بدلونه
- Please double-check: In Pakistani Pashto mathematical prose, is «يو شان ځايناستي / هممهاله ځايناستي» the clearest conventional rendering of “uniform substitution / simultaneous substitution” while preserving the distinctions in the rationale? If not, what exact replacement and inflected forms should be used?

## TERM-AXIOM

- Source term or sense: axiom / axiom schema
- Choice: بديهي اصل / د بديهي اصل شېما
- Why provisional: axiom and schema concepts regionally attested; Pakistani surface adapted
- Rationale: An axiom is an adopted starting sentence; an axiom schema represents all its substitution instances. Keep individual axioms distinct from the schema that generates them and from premises supplied by a theory.
- Alternatives: اکسيوم; منل شوے اصل
- Please double-check: In Pakistani Pashto mathematical prose, is «بديهي اصل / د بديهي اصل شېما» the clearest conventional rendering of “axiom / axiom schema” while preserving the distinctions in the rationale? If not, what exact replacement and inflected forms should be used?

## TERM-LOGIC-FORMS

- Source term or sense: formula / derivation / propositional logic / first-order logic / completeness
- Choice: فارمول / اشتقاق / بياني منطق / د لومړۍ درجې منطق / بشپړتيا
- Why provisional: regional subject, formula and proof vocabulary; derivation/completeness wording provisional
- Rationale: A formula is an object-language expression; a derivation is a finite syntactic witness governed by a particular proof system. Completeness relates semantic consequence to derivability and must remain distinct from real-number completeness.
- Alternatives: صيغه د فارمول پر ځاے; استنتاج د اشتقاق پر ځاے
- Please double-check: In Pakistani Pashto mathematical prose, is «فارمول / اشتقاق / بياني منطق / د لومړۍ درجې منطق / بشپړتيا» the clearest conventional rendering of “formula / derivation / propositional logic / first-order logic / completeness” while preserving the distinctions in the rationale? If not, what exact replacement and inflected forms should be used?

## TERM-PROOF-SYSTEMS

- Source term or sense: derivation system / derivation / theorem / derivable / inference rule / proof
- Choice: د اشتقاق نظام / اشتقاق / قضيه / د اشتقاق وړ / د استنتاج قاعده / ثبوت
- Why provisional: proof and inference concepts regionally attested; derivation label is an editorial distinction
- Rationale: Use اشتقاق for the finite formal object and ثبوت for the broader mathematical proof or act of proving. A theorem has a derivation without undischarged external premises; derivability from Gamma depends on the selected system and must not be conflated with semantic entailment.
- Alternatives: د ثبوت نظام او صوري ثبوت; استدلالي حساب
- Please double-check: In Pakistani Pashto mathematical prose, is «د اشتقاق نظام / اشتقاق / قضيه / د اشتقاق وړ / د استنتاج قاعده / ثبوت» the clearest conventional rendering of “derivation system / derivation / theorem / derivable / inference rule / proof” while preserving the distinctions in the rationale? If not, what exact replacement and inflected forms should be used?

## TERM-PROOF-PROPERTIES

- Source term or sense: soundness / completeness / consistency / inconsistency / validity
- Choice: سموالے / بشپړتيا / سازګاري / ناسازګاري / اعتبار
- Why provisional: validity, contradiction and consistency concepts regionally attested; full property set adapted
- Rationale: Soundness sends derivability to semantic consequence; completeness sends semantic consequence to derivability. Consistency is syntactic and satisfiability semantic, even when soundness and completeness later make the corresponding conditions equivalent.
- Alternatives: صحت او تکميل; نه تناقض او اعتبار
- Please double-check: In Pakistani Pashto mathematical prose, is «سموالے / بشپړتيا / سازګاري / ناسازګاري / اعتبار» the clearest conventional rendering of “soundness / completeness / consistency / inconsistency / validity” while preserving the distinctions in the rationale? If not, what exact replacement and inflected forms should be used?

## TERM-PROOF-CALCULI

- Source term or sense: sequent / sequent calculus / natural deduction / assumption / discharge / tableau / signed formula / axiomatic derivation / modus ponens
- Choice: سېکوېنټ / د سېکوېنټ حساب / طبيعي استنتاج / فرض / د فرض ختمول / تابلو / نښه لرونکے فارمول / بديهي اشتقاق / مودوس پوننس
- Why provisional: axiomatic proof and modus ponens attested; specialized calculus labels provisional
- Rationale: Keep the four proof formats distinct: sequents arrange two sentence sequences, natural deduction tracks discharged assumptions, tableaux branch on signed formulas, and axiomatic derivations are linear sequences justified by axioms, premises or inference rules.
- Alternatives: نتيجوي حساب او فطري استنتاج; رښتيا ونه او اکسيوماتيک ثبوت
- Please double-check: In Pakistani Pashto mathematical prose, is «سېکوېنټ / د سېکوېنټ حساب / طبيعي استنتاج / فرض / د فرض ختمول / تابلو / نښه لرونکے فارمول / بديهي اشتقاق / مودوس پوننس» the clearest conventional rendering of “sequent / sequent calculus / natural deduction / assumption / discharge / tableau / signed formula / axiomatic derivation / modus ponens” while preserving the distinctions in the rationale? If not, what exact replacement and inflected forms should be used?

## TERM-SEQUENT-STRUCTURE

- Source term or sense: antecedent / succedent / initial sequent / logical rule / structural rule / main operator
- Choice: مقدم / تالي / لومړنے سېکوېنټ / منطقي قاعده / جوړښتي قاعده / اصلي عملګر
- Why provisional: sequent concept regionally supported by proof prose; specialized component labels provisional
- Rationale: The antecedent is the finite sequence on the left and the succedent the finite sequence on the right. An initial sequent begins a derivation. Logical rules act on a displayed main operator, while structural rules rearrange, duplicate, remove or add sequence members without introducing a connective.
- Alternatives: مخکښ او وروستۍ برخه; ابتدايي سېکوېنټ او قاعدوي نښلون
- Please double-check: In Pakistani Pashto mathematical prose, is «مقدم / تالي / لومړنے سېکوېنټ / منطقي قاعده / جوړښتي قاعده / اصلي عملګر» the clearest conventional rendering of “antecedent / succedent / initial sequent / logical rule / structural rule / main operator” while preserving the distinctions in the rationale? If not, what exact replacement and inflected forms should be used?

## TERM-QUANTIFIER-RULES

- Source term or sense: universal quantifier / existential quantifier / closed term / free variable / bound variable
- Choice: عمومي سور / وجودي سور / تړلې اصطلاح / ازاد متغير / تړلے متغير
- Why provisional: predicate and open-formula context regionally attested; exact quantifier compounds provisional
- Rationale: A closed term contains no variables. The universal and existential rule schemas retain the source distinction between free occurrences in A(x) and the bound variable introduced by the quantifier; Pashto prose must not weaken those occurrence conditions.
- Alternatives: کلي او وجودي کميت ټاکونکي; تړلے ترم او ايګن متغير
- Please double-check: In Pakistani Pashto mathematical prose, is «عمومي سور / وجودي سور / تړلې اصطلاح / ازاد متغير / تړلے متغير» the clearest conventional rendering of “universal quantifier / existential quantifier / closed term / free variable / bound variable” while preserving the distinctions in the rationale? If not, what exact replacement and inflected forms should be used?

## TERM-SEQUENT-RULES

- Source term or sense: eigenvariable / eigenvariable condition / weakening / contraction / exchange / cut
- Choice: ځانګړے متغير / د ځانګړي متغير شرط / کمزوري کول / انقباض / تبادله / پرېکون
- Why provisional: inference-rule concepts supported; exact sequent-rule labels unattested and provisional
- Rationale: Keep the historical eigenvariable name even where the rule uses a constant, and state its lower-sequent freshness condition exactly. Weakening adds a sentence, contraction merges duplicates, exchange swaps adjacent positions, and cut combines derivations across the same cut formula.
- Alternatives: اضافه او اختصار; تعويض او قطع
- Please double-check: In Pakistani Pashto mathematical prose, is «ځانګړے متغير / د ځانګړي متغير شرط / کمزوري کول / انقباض / تبادله / پرېکون» the clearest conventional rendering of “eigenvariable / eigenvariable condition / weakening / contraction / exchange / cut” while preserving the distinctions in the rationale? If not, what exact replacement and inflected forms should be used?

## TERM-DERIVATION-SEARCH

- Source term or sense: end-sequent / topmost sequent / bottommost sequent / premise / conclusion / backward proof search / rule instance
- Choice: وروستنے سېکوېنټ / تر ټولو بره سېکوېنټ / تر ټولو لاندې سېکوېنټ / مقدمه / پايله / له پايلې څخه شاته د ثبوت لټون / د قاعدې کارونه
- Why provisional: proof and conclusion language regionally supported; exact sequent-tree labels and backward-search compound provisional
- Rationale: A finite derivation tree is oriented with initial sequents at the top and its end-sequent at the bottom. Backward proof search begins with that end-sequent and matches it exactly to a rule conclusion, then continues upward through the corresponding premises.
- Alternatives: وروستۍ نتيجه او مخکينۍ مقدمې; له لاندې څخه پورته قاعدوي تجزيه
- Please double-check: In Pakistani Pashto mathematical prose, is «وروستنے سېکوېنټ / تر ټولو بره سېکوېنټ / تر ټولو لاندې سېکوېنټ / مقدمه / پايله / له پايلې څخه شاته د ثبوت لټون / د قاعدې کارونه» the clearest conventional rendering of “end-sequent / topmost sequent / bottommost sequent / premise / conclusion / backward proof search / rule instance” while preserving the distinctions in the rationale? If not, what exact replacement and inflected forms should be used?

## TERM-DERIVABILITY-PROPERTIES

- Source term or sense: proof-theoretic / reflexivity / monotonicity / transitivity / compactness (finite witness) / finitely satisfiable / Compactness Theorem
- Choice: ثبوت-تيوريکي / انعکاسيت / يکنواختي / انتقاليت / د متناهي شاهد خاصيت / په متناهي ډول د صدق وړ / د متناهي شاهد قضيه
- Why provisional: proof, consistency, satisfiability and relational concepts regionally supported; exact proof-theoretic and compactness compounds provisional
- Rationale: The proof-theoretic compactness property says a derivation or inconsistency already has a finite witness. The semantic Compactness Theorem says a set is satisfiable when every finite subset is satisfiable, equivalently that semantic entailment already has a finite premise set. The edition uses one finite-witness word family while keeping syntactic consistency distinct from semantic satisfiability.
- Alternatives: د ثبوت اړوند مفاهيم; انعکاس، زياتېدنه، انتقال او تراکم; د تراکم قضيه; په متناهي ډول پوره کېدونکی

If not, what exact replacement and inflected forms should be used?

## TERM-SEQUENT-SOUNDNESS

- Source term or sense: sequent validity / sound derivation system / induction on derivation / lowest inference / semantic preservation
- Choice: د سېکوېنټ اعتبار / سم اشتقاقي نظام / پر اشتقاق استقرا / تر ټولو لاندې استنتاج / د معنايي اعتبار ساتنه
- Why provisional: semantic and proof roles regionally supported; exact multiword sequent-soundness compounds provisional
- Rationale: Validity means that every interpretation satisfies the sequent. Soundness is proved by induction on derivation height or inference count, checking that each lowest rule preserves semantic validity from its premises to its conclusion.
- Alternatives: د سېکوېنټ صحت; د معنا ساتونکے استنتاج; پر ثبوت استقرا

## TERM-NATURAL-DEDUCTION

- Source term or sense: natural deduction / assumption / discharge / introduction rule / elimination rule / premise / conclusion
- Choice: فطري استنتاج / فرضيه / فرضيه ايستل / د داخلولو قاعده / د ايستلو قاعده / مقدمه / پايله
- Why provisional: proof-system and premise/conclusion roles regionally supported; exact natural-deduction and discharge compounds provisional
- Rationale: Natural deduction is presented as a tree system beginning from assumptions. Introduction and elimination rules add or remove the principal connective, and labeled discharge closes selected assumption occurrences while leaving other assumptions open.

## TERM-TABLEAUX

- Source term or sense: tableau / signed formula / truth sign / branch / root / closed and open branch / cut / eigenvariable condition
- Choice: تابلو / نښه لرونکے فارمول / د رښتياوالي نښه / څانګه / ريښه / تړلې او پرانيستې څانګه / پرېکون / د ځانګړي متغير شرط
- Why provisional: proof, truth, semantic and predicate roles regionally supported; exact tableau, signed-formula and branch compounds provisional
- Rationale: A tableau is a downward-growing tree of signed formulas. A branch closes only when it contains matching true and false signs for the same formula. Branching rules create alternatives; nonbranching rules extend one path. Cut splits on an arbitrary signed formula, and quantified witness rules retain the exact closed-term and eigenvariable restrictions.
- Alternatives: رښتيا ونه; لاسليک لرونکے فارمول; خلاصه او بنده څانګه; د قضيو وېش

If not, what exact replacement and inflected forms should be used?

## TERM-COMPLETENESS-CONSTRUCTION

- Source term or sense: complete consistent set / saturated set / Henkin expansion / Lindenbaum lemma / term model / Truth Lemma / factored term model
- Choice: بشپړ سازګار سټ / مشبوع سټ / د هنکين غځونه / د لينډنباوم لمه / ترمي مدل / د صدق لمه / خارج قسمت شوے ترمي مدل
- Why provisional: general semantic, equality, relation, consistency, induction and proof concepts attested; exact construction and lemma labels unattested and provisional
- Rationale: A complete consistent set decides every sentence while remaining syntactically consistent. Saturation supplies quantified witnesses; Lindenbaum extension completes the set; the term model uses closed terms as its domain. The Truth Lemma equates satisfaction with membership. When identity is present, the factored term model identifies terms whose equality belongs to the complete set and interprets symbols on the resulting equivalence classes. The compact Pashto construction labels are edition choices; cited Afghan works are regional comparators.
- Alternatives: تام سازګار سټ; اشباع شوے سټ; د هنکين توسعه; د لينډنباوم فرعي قضيه; د اصطلاحاتو مدل; د رښتياوالي لمه; د هم ارزښتۍ پر ټولګيو جوړ مدل

## TERM-FOL-SYNTAX

- Source term or sense: first-order language / constant / variable / term / formula / sentence / quantifier / scope / atomic formula
- Choice: د لومړۍ درجې ژبه / ثابت / متغير / ترم / فارمول / جمله / کميت ټاکونکے / ساحه / اټومي فارمول
- Why provisional: predicate, variable and formula contexts regionally attested; the grouped Pakistani terminology remains provisional
- Rationale: Constants name individual objects, variables receive assignment values, and terms denote objects. Predicates applied to terms form atomic formulas; connectives and quantifiers form further formulas. A sentence is exactly a formula with no free variable occurrence, and quantifier scope and binding must remain distinct from mere symbol occurrence.
- Alternatives: د محمولاتو منطق ژبه; فردي ثابت او فردي متغير; صيغه د فارمول پر ځاے; د کميت ټاکونکي د عمل ساحه

## TERM-VARIABLE-ASSIGNMENT

- Source term or sense: variable assignment / modified assignment / value of a term / assignment independence
- Choice: د متغير ګومارنه / بدله ګومارنه / د ترم قيمت / له ګومارنې خپلواکي
- Why provisional: mapping, value and satisfaction concepts supported; exact variable-assignment compounds are provisional
- Rationale: A variable assignment maps every variable into the structure domain. Its x-modification changes only x. Satisfaction of a formula depends only on its free variables, so satisfaction of a sentence is independent of the assignment. Keep this semantic assignment distinct from object-language substitution.
- Alternatives: د متغير ارزښت ټاکنه; بدله ارزښت ټاکنه; له ارزښت ټاکنې خپلواکي
- Please double-check: In Pakistani Pashto mathematical prose, is «د متغير ګومارنه / بدله ګومارنه / د ترم قيمت / له ګومارنې خپلواکي» the clearest conventional rendering of “variable assignment / modified assignment / value of a term / assignment independence” while preserving the distinctions in the rationale? If not, what exact replacement and inflected forms should be used?

## TERM-STRUCTURAL-INDUCTION

- Source term or sense: inductive definition / structural induction / atomic base / generation clause / limiting clause
- Choice: استقرايي تعريف / جوړښتي استقرا / اټومي بنسټ / جوړوونکے شرط / محدودوونکے شرط
- Why provisional: induction and formula-generation concepts regionally supported; exact structural compounds are provisional
- Rationale: The atomic clauses supply the base objects, formation clauses close the class under specified constructors, and the limiting clause excludes every other string. Structural induction proves a property by the matching base and constructor cases; it is not induction on a numerical index unless a separate coding is supplied.
- Alternatives: ساختماني استقرا; د فارمول پر جوړښت استقرا; توليدي او د بندولو شرطونه
- Please double-check: In Pakistani Pashto mathematical prose, is «استقرايي تعريف / جوړښتي استقرا / اټومي بنسټ / جوړوونکے شرط / محدودوونکے شرط» the clearest conventional rendering of “inductive definition / structural induction / atomic base / generation clause / limiting clause” while preserving the distinctions in the rationale? If not, what exact replacement and inflected forms should be used?

## TERM-MODEL-THEORY

- Source term or sense: model / theory / model theory / axiomatic method / characterize / preorder
- Choice: مدل / تيوري / مدل تيوري / بديهي طريقه / ځانګړې کول / مخکښ‌مرتبه
- Why provisional: theory, relation, order, entailment and countability concepts supported; model-theoretic compounds are provisional
- Rationale: A model of Gamma is a structure satisfying every sentence in Gamma. A theory axiomatizes a class when its models are exactly that class. The preorder example requires reflexivity and transitivity together. Compactness and Löwenheim--Skolem limit which domain-size classes first-order theories can characterize.
- Alternatives: د مدلونو تيوري; اکسيوماتيکي طريقه; مقدماتي ترتيب د مخکښ‌مرتبې پر ځاے
- Please double-check: In Pakistani Pashto mathematical prose, is «مدل / تيوري / مدل تيوري / بديهي طريقه / ځانګړې کول / مخکښ‌مرتبه» the clearest conventional rendering of “model / theory / model theory / axiomatic method / characterize / preorder” while preserving the distinctions in the rationale? If not, what exact replacement and inflected forms should be used?

## TERM-LOGIC-ORDER-HIERARCHY

- Source term or sense: many-sorted logic / second-order logic / higher-order logic
- Choice: د ډېرو ډولونو منطق / د دويمې درجې منطق / د لوړې درجې منطق
- Why provisional: supporting logic, predicate, relation and semantic concepts regionally attested; exact hierarchy labels unattested and provisional
- Rationale: Sorts separate object domains. Second-order variables range over relations on the first-order domain, while higher-order systems iterate function or relation types. The edition uses one transparent order vocabulary and does not treat these three systems as interchangeable.
- Alternatives: څو ډوله منطق; ثانوي منطق; عالي مرتبه منطق
- Please double-check: In Pakistani Pashto mathematical prose, is «د ډېرو ډولونو منطق / د دويمې درجې منطق / د لوړې درجې منطق» the clearest conventional rendering of “many-sorted logic / second-order logic / higher-order logic” while preserving the distinctions in the rationale? If not, what exact replacement and inflected forms should be used?

## TERM-SECOND-ORDER-SEMANTICS

- Source term or sense: comprehension schema / predicative and impredicative / full and weak second-order semantics / categorical
- Choice: د ادراک شېما / وړاندوينيز او ناوړاندوينيز / بشپړه او کمزورې د دويمې درجې معنٰی پوهنه / قطعي
- Why provisional: supporting axiom, relation and semantics concepts regionally attested; exact second-order compounds unattested and provisional
- Rationale: Comprehension supplies a relation corresponding to a formula. Predicative comprehension excludes second-order quantifiers from that defining formula; impredicative comprehension allows them. Full semantics quantifies over every relation of the relevant arity, while weak semantics supplies a selected relation domain. Categorical means unique up to isomorphism, not merely detailed.
- Alternatives: د جامع والي شېما; پريډيکاټيف او امپريډيکاټيف; معياري او هنکين معنٰی پوهنه; کټګوريکي د قطعي پر ځاے

## TERM-MODEL-THEORY-STRUCTURES

- Source term or sense: reduct / expansion / substructure / theory of a structure / elementary equivalence / automorphism
- Choice: راکمونه / غځونه / فرعي جوړښت / د جوړښت تيوري / ابتدايي هم‌ارز / اتومورفيزم
- Why provisional: structure, theory, interpretation, relation and function concepts supported by regional sources; exact model-theoretic compounds and the automorphism label are provisional
- Rationale: A reduct forgets symbols while retaining the shared interpretations, an expansion adds interpretations, a substructure closes the inherited operations on a smaller domain, and the theory of a structure collects its true sentences. Elementary equivalence concerns the same sentences; isomorphism is stronger, and an automorphism maps a structure onto itself. The edition keeps these roles distinct.
- Alternatives: راکمونه او غځونې; فرعي جوړښت او پراخ جوړښت; د جوړښت د رښتينو جملو تيوري; ساختماني يو شان والی او ځان‌نقشه

## TERM-PARTIAL-ISOMORPHISM

- Source term or sense: partial isomorphism / back-and-forth / quantifier rank / n-equivalence
- Choice: جزوي آيزومورفيزم / مخ او شا / د کميت ټاکونکو رتبه / n-هم‌ارز
- Why provisional: partial, relation, quantifier and induction concepts supported by regional sources; exact back-and-forth and quantifier-rank compounds are provisional
- Rationale: A partial isomorphism is a finite injective map preserving the relevant constants, predicates and functions on its domain. Back-and-forth extends its domain and range alternately. Quantifier rank counts nested quantifiers, while n-equivalence compares sentences up to that rank. The edition does not collapse these notions into ordinary equivalence.
- Alternatives: نيمګړے آيزومورفيزم; مخکې او شاته غځونه; د کميت ټاکونکو ژورتيا; n-برابرښت

## TERM-OVERSPILL

- Source term or sense: overspill / arbitrarily large finite models / infinite model

- Rationale: The overspill argument expands the language by pairwise-distinct constants.
